each occupied class keeps its own lexicase choice among the elite, and half of all children have a parent chosen within a class drawn at random, so small and large networks both stay in the population]) with a wiring cost (ISL_WIRE_COST=1: each step a genome pays 1 for each bit of Hamming distance between the tags of the two ends of each of its populations, ports at fixed tags in the same space, as the carrying cost is paid; near ends cheap, far ones dear, so circuits that work together gather in one part of tag space near the ports they use), populations competing for their destination (ISL_COMPETE=1: the populations into a destination run strongest grip first, ties in genome order, and each word an earlier population's kernels wrote this step is claimed, and a kernel whose output words are mostly claimed is held down and learns anti-Hebbian, so the winners write the act, not the OR of every reflex) and pruning (ISL_PRUNE=1: each population holds its destination with its gene grip, 16 to start, up one a step it writes, down one a step it only loses, letting go for the lifetime at 0);
